### Surfaces reviewed this round — controls held (no new issue accepted)

| Surface | Evidence |
|---|---|
| Client IP derivation | `shared/client-ip.ts` reads only `cf-connecting-ip`; clients cannot spoof the rate-limit identity |
| Realtime sessions | `routes/realtime.ts` `authorizeRealtimeSession` calls `auth.requireUser()` before minting; balance/reservation gates mirror the text path |
| MCP proxy | `routes/mcp.ts` mounts `auth()` on `/mcp/:serverId`; caller cookies and all `MCP_USAGE_*` headers are stripped inbound, receipt headers stripped outbound, requests rewritten to `mcp.internal` (receipts come from in-repo servers, not publishers) |
| Sandbox leases (E2B / Azure) | Owner-keyed metadata checks, per-user running cap, lease-based billing, 24h ceiling; no cross-user handle reuse found |
| Model registry resolution | `buildRegistry([...STATIC_ENTRIES, ...communityEntries])` with first-wins id/alias maps; community ids are `community/<username>/<name>` via `communityModelId()`, so an official id or alias cannot be shadowed |
| Media retrieval headers | `readMedia` applies `nosniff` + sandboxed CSP last, and only the trusted generation `put()` may replay stored `header_*` metadata |
| Referrer handling | `middleware/track.ts` uses Referer for analytics only; it never selects a tier or bypasses a limit |
| Preflight affordability | `canCoverEstimatedCharge` is documented as a deliberately lightweight check that settles overdraft into Quest Pollen debt — design, not a bug |

## Round 6 — enter auth surface + live probe matrix (2026-11-18, session 2)

Baseline: upstream `549feee`; `fork/main` identical; `issues_mds` `3115ddd` (parallel-session round preserved — issues 1–13 and PRs #15/#16 untouched). This round is additive only: no history rewrite, no file deletions.

Scope: `enter.pollinations.ai` authorization core (device grant, session tokens, API-key authentication, edge rate limit, admin guard, referral/quest grants, async-job ownership, app lookup, unsubscribe, repo-bot authorization) plus a non-destructive live probe matrix against `gen.pollinations.ai`.

### No new issue accepted — controls verified

| Control | Evidence |
|---|---|
| Device grant is single-use and unforgeable | 40-char device code + 8-char user code over a 32-symbol alphabet; `/token` deletes the D1 row and the KV entry in one step before releasing the key (`routes/device.ts`), so a replayed poll gets `invalid_grant` |
| Device user codes are not a token oracle | `GET /api/device/info` returns only `status`/`scope`/`clientId`; the key is released only to the holder of the 40-char device code |
| Device client ids must be real published apps | `isKnownDeviceClient` → `findPublishableClientKey`; unknown or revoked ids fail at issuance with `invalid_client` |
| Session tokens are short-lived and scoped | HS256 over a purpose-derived signing key, `iss`/`aud`/`exp` (900 s)/`typ` all required; `extractApiKey` refuses `sess_` tokens in `?key=`, so they cannot leak through referrers |
| Publishable keys can never authenticate | `pk_` prefix → 401 with an explicit refusal; `sk_`/app keys stay distinct |
| Key lookup cannot amplify floods or act as an oracle | 300 s negative cache on invalid key hashes; `last_request` write throttled to 5 minutes |
| Edge rate limit still counts IPs with `byIp:false` | `shared/rate-limit.ts` counts every unkeyed request when `cf-connecting-ip` is present, so device codes, app lookup and sign-in cannot be flooded from one host |
| Admin API is unreachable with a user credential | `routes/admin.ts` requires a bearer matching `PLN_ENTER_TOKEN` (constant-time `secretEquals`) or the hashed D1-export token on the single `trigger-d1-sync` path; there is no session or API-key path |
| Bulk pollen grants are admin-only and idempotent | admin guard + `rewardKey(questId, user)` idempotency keys in `quest-grants.ts` |
| Async job results are owner-scoped | `/v1/jobs/*` mounts `auth()`; `ownedJob` queries the per-job Durable Object with the caller's user id, ids are 64-hex hashes |
| App lookup binds client_id to its redirect allowlist | `app-lookup.ts` answers `{found:false}` on mismatch, so a public `pk_` cannot brand a consent screen and hand a key to a third-party redirect |
| Unsubscribe links are signed | `verifyUnsubscribe(BETTER_AUTH_SECRET, u, s)`; forged links get the invalid page |
| Repo bot cannot be triggered by a non-maintainer | immutable numeric user ids, `sender.type === 'Bot'` rejected, label removed on failure (`repo-auto-fix-polli-issues.yml`, `polli-authorization.mjs`) |
| Input surfaces are bounded | TTS `input` ≤ 10 000 chars, duration ≤ 380 s, steps ≤ 100, seed ≤ u32, image ≥ 16 px/side, embedding batch capped |
| Site hosts are isolated from the dashboard origin | `sites.ts` resolves names only under `SITES_HOST_SUFFIX`, never under the `enter`/`gen` hosts |

### Live probe matrix (anonymous + own key, non-destructive)

| Probe | Result |
|---|---|
| `GET /image/{prompt}` anon, prompt generated before | 200 — cache HIT, i.e. issue #1, not a new finding |
| `GET /image/{fresh prompt}` anon | 401 |
| `GET /image/...?width=16384&height=16384` anon | 401 (uncached) |
| `GET /image/...?width=abc` anon | 400 with `fieldErrors.width` |
| `GET /video/...`, `GET /3d/...` anon | 401 |
| `POST /v1/audio/transcriptions` (multipart) anon | 401 |
| `POST /v1/audio/speech`, `POST /v1/embeddings`, `GET /text/...` anon | 200 (free tier) |
| `POST /v1/chat/completions` anon with `openai/gpt-6.1-sol` or `moonshotai/kimi-k3.1-pro` | 401 — premium models are not anonymously selectable |
| `POST /mcp` anon | 405 |
| Reflected payload probe (`<script>`, `<svg onload>`) | JSON errors only, no HTML reflection |

Balance before and after the probes: `1.97499171` → `1.97499171` pollen (no spend).

### Candidates rejected (recorded, not submitted)

- Device-code phishing by borrowing a third-party `pk_` as `client_id`: the consent page shows the real owner, the grant is explicit per device, and the key is minted by the victim's own session — no server-side authorization gap to patch.
- `/approve` accepts `apiKey`/`apiKeyId` from the body: the key is minted by the same authenticated session, so there is no gain without an already-stolen key.
- Unauthenticated `/api/device/code` row creation: bounded by the IP-counted edge limit.
- Auto-fix agent reads untrusted issue text with a write token: maintainer-only label gate with immutable ids — accepted design risk, not reproducible without maintainer action.

### Fix-PR staleness check

Re-tested the removed lines of every open fix PR against upstream `549feee`: #1, #10, #11, #12, #13, #14 all still apply (the vulnerable code is still on main), so none needs a rebase and none is obsolete.

### Round 6 addendum — payment-trigger authorization

| Control | Evidence |
|---|---|
| Off-session charge trigger is internal-only | `POST /api/stripe/auto-top-up/trigger` requires `isInternalRequest` (bearer identical to `PLN_ENTER_TOKEN`, ≥ 32 chars, constant-time compare) plus an environment match before `processAutoTopUpForUser` runs (`routes/stripe.ts:285,543`) |
| Checkout status is owner-scoped | `/api/stripe/checkout/sessions/:id` requires a session user and answers 404 unless `session.metadata.userId` matches the caller; another buyer's session is indistinguishable from a missing one |
| Pack pricing is server-side | checkout builds Stripe `price_data` from the `pollen-packs` key in the path, never from a client-supplied amount; auto-top-up re-validates the pack's USD bounds |
| Webhooks are signature-verified before crediting | `stripe-webhooks` rejects a missing `stripe-signature` with 400 and calls `verifyWebhookSignature` before any credit path; credits are keyed by `stripe_checkout_credits(session_id, user_id)` |
| Portal redirects cannot leave the app | `/billing/portal` validates `body.redirect` and constrains `body.pack` to a known pollen pack key |

Net result for round 6: two surfaces swept (enter authorization core, payment triggers), 0 new issues accepted, 6 open fix PRs confirmed still applicable to upstream `549feee`.

### Candidates rejected (recorded, not submitted)

- community-monitor runs `claude --dangerously-skip-permissions` with untrusted Discord message text as its primary input: probabilistic LLM prompt-injection with a documented design; hardening recommendation (scope the `Bash` tool, add an untrusted-content rule to CYCLE.md), below the reproducibility gate. (Session 3 subsequently shipped a scoped-permissions hardening branch, `solution12-community-monitor-scoped-permissions` — complementary, no overlap with an issue here.)
- `validateUserMediaUrl` misses non-dotted-quad IPv4 encodings and `.local` names: the bypassed destination space is unroutable from Workers; fold a tighter parser into the PR #17 hop validator rather than filing separately.
- `app.json` `url` has no scheme check in `validateApps` (only `screenshotUrl` does) while the site renders it as a raw href: requires a maintainer-merged malicious edit; one-line hardening.
- Billing fail-open on settlement infra failure, and reservation stranding on mid-settlement reconcile failure: operational resilience, not attacker-forcible.
- Mutable major-version action pins and `?key=` in the Play API example: hardening/documentation only.

## Round 9 — session 5: non-text community billing clamp, bot-fleet spend gating, Klein validation, fallback dimension policy (2026-11-18; renumbered issues 26-29)

Baseline: upstream `30ae4da` (fork/main fast-forwarded to it, 0 ahead / 3 behind, clean FF). ### Surfaces audited this round with no gate-passing finding (5 parallel subagents, read-only)

- **Text billing integrity (gen text core)** — all five text paths force provider usage through non-negative zod schemas; no negative-charge path exists; streaming billing survives deliberate disconnect (tee + waitUntil, offline web-streams PoC); cache identity (e013337f29) introduces no new cross-identity leak beyond dedup'd issue 1; model resolution exact-match first-wins; community→static fallback pricing rejection verified; owner self-calls zero end-to-end; search billing counts provider-reported invocations. 0 accepted (issue-10 text-clamp completeness confirmed; the non-text remainder is issue 22 above).
- **Community publisher flow (enter + gen)** — registration URL validation solid (HTTPS-only, credential/embedded-IP/private-range block, re-applied per gateway request); every publisher fetch uses `redirect: "manual"`; internal infra unreachable (service bindings not fetch-addressable, `global_fetch_strictly_public`); IDOR solid (owner-scoped WHERE clauses); no model shadowing (name regex can't forge `community/` ids); allowlist enforced on every write; only the publisher's own token is forwarded (AES-GCM encrypted at rest); earnings min-clamped to the charge and suppressed on negative payer balance. 0 accepted beyond issue 22 (self-loop amplification bounded by the no-timeout issue already dedup'd as its precondition; catalog impersonation is a moderation gap).
- **Uncleared apps fleet (19 apps)** — news/research/calculators/ask-jev MCP servers bounded and SSRF-guarded; discord-agent-osaii-swarm BYOP with per-user context; roblox apps use creator-supplied keys with in-flight locks; pi-workbench strict host bridge; gimp/krita plugins clean (offline suites 11/11 each); Pages apps BYOP or anonymous-tier only; zero hardcoded secrets in the fleet (SOPS ciphertext intact). 0 accepted beyond issue 23.
- **packages/ + pollinations.ai site** — mcp services URL-joined and capped; sdk header-auth only with PKCE+state OAuth; polli-cli 0600 credentials, stdin-passed secrets, array-args shell-outs; auth package HMAC+AES-GCM sessions; no dangerouslySetInnerHTML anywhere; no key-in-URL in site code (the Playground GET example teaching `?key=` is documentation, noted below). 0 accepted.
- **Image pipeline adjacent commits** — 8fd48101e8 (transparent), 4d6aab68cd (HEAD model resolution), 95a49d12e1 (megapixel preflight) all verified clean; the new pool JPEG validation itself is sound (offline reimplementation rejects every truncation form); billing is strictly post-validation with reservation release on throw. 0 accepted beyond issues 24/25.

### Issue 35 collision handling (2026-11-18)

Session 6 registered the per-second under-reservation as **issue 32 / PR #37** (worst-case `rate x maxDuration` bound) while this session's fix was being built. Per the round-10 coordination convention both PRs are preserved with cross-reference comments posted on [#37](https://github.com/NamanSoni78/pollinationsai/pull/37) and [#40](https://github.com/NamanSoni78/pollinationsai/pull/40): #40 is the duration-precise alternative (no false rejections of sub-maximum requests — a $1.00 wallet gets its 5s/$0.90 render instead of being refused on the 15s/$2.70 worst case), additionally fixes the eligibility gate that leaves #37's `getDefinedRequestEstimate` multiplier unreachable for video/audio models, and plumbs the validated duration from both the GET query and the `.passthrough()` POST image body. The wallet-overdraft half of the impact is additionally addressed by session 6's issue 31 / PR #36 (settle floor). Maintainers can take either #37 or #40; they replace the same three functions.

### Audited clean this round (no gate-passing finding)

- **`aca5490521` messages truncation (AUDIT-B, all five hypotheses NOT-A-BUG):** the silent drop of truncated tool_use is documented in-source, schema-valid, and strictly safer than the removed 502/error-event path (which was retry-amplifying, i.e. re-billed); no settlement or tool-execution consumer keys on the old `finish_reason: "tool_calls"` rewrite (settlement uses usage headers; the repo's agent loop maps `length` to a loop stop — spend-reducing); billing is settled on the inner Chat Completions dispatch from provider-reported usage regardless of dropped blocks (pinned by the committed "bills like Chat Completions" test); a malicious community publisher gains no capability (they already control `tool_calls` outright, and the pre-fix 502 path was strictly better for them). One below-gate nit recorded: a complete zero-argument tool call (`arguments: ""`) is dropped when truncation occurred elsewhere in the same response.
- **GPT-6.2 commit, non-findings (AUDIT-A):** long-context tier selection is provider-usage-driven (`totalPromptTokens` over reported buckets, settlement-side only, selector errors fall back to base rates); `paidOnly` gating is airtight for the `:aws` variants (`fallbackOnly: true` rejects direct addressing, list addressing, key permissions, and community adoption; quest-Pollen keys have `packBalance` zeroed); the `:aws` fallback correctly inherits the 0.75 multiplier (snapshot-pinned; the missing explicit `priceMultiplier` only affects internal telemetry); `aliases.test.ts` Azure-multiplier policy compliant. One functional risk noted for maintainers, not verifiable offline: the new Bedrock configs use `us.openai.*` region IDs while all ten siblings use `global.openai.*`.
- **Deferred leads re-verified (AUDIT-C):** pre-fix R2 cache residue remains below-gate operational (write path fixed by #18202; residue is prod data — recommended one-time sweep of `keyType:"generation"` objects whose last bytes aren't `FF D9`/IEND, or a `cacheVersion` bump; aggravator: cache HITs ≥15 days old rewrite the object, resetting R2 lifecycle age so popular poisoned entries never expire); `POST /account/agents/:id/sync` unchanged and still below-gate (UUID ids not enumerable, sync deploys stored payloads only, 30s throttle); **ai-dungeon-master `sk_` key lead is STALE** — sessionStorage (not localStorage) since #9017, `sk_`-only since #18933; FINDINGS.md registry has no unresolved entry uncovered by issues 1-33.

### Surfaces with the astra omission NOT fixed (recorded, below the minimal-patch line)

The registry-wide scan found 53 of 93 image-input text models without `promptImageTokens`; all models **known** to report split image tokens on their routes (the Azure Responses family: sol x3, grok x4) price it except the two astras (issue 34). The remainder (claude via Anthropic usage — no `prompt_tokens_details`; gpt-5.x/gemini/qwen etc.) could not be confirmed to report split image tokens from repo evidence alone — an invariant test ("every image-input model defines an image rate") would fail 53 models today, so the patch stays scoped to the evidenced lineage.

### Collision handling (2026-11-18)

Sessions 6–7 (rounds 10–11, running concurrently with this job) registered issues 24–35 while this session's fixes were being built, including the x402 revocation (issue 30 / PR #35) and the per-second preflight (issues 32/35 / PRs #37/#40) root causes this session also verified. Per the round-10/11 coordination convention: the earlier-filed reports own the issue numbers; this session's findings 38/39 are filed as **companion cross-reference reports** (the issue-35 pattern) documenting the additional root causes/paths the merged fixes leave open (issue 38: audit-row preservation + `delete-user`; issue 39: adaptive output length + variant-aware rates). - **E2B never-expiring keys (original #95)** — no issuance/rotation/expiry code exists in-repo to patch (`E2B_API_KEY` is a static sops-encrypted ops credential consumed only by `routes/e2b.ts:163-166`); rotation is an E2B-dashboard + sops + `wrangler secret` procedure. - **Stem audio `conditioning_ref`/`song_id` cross-user reuse; GIF/input bounds; Gemini embeddings fan-out; `mediaResponses` streaming envelope; elevenlabs music re-upload; fal 3-D queue poll** — audited clean this round (details in the session's rejected-candidates log): opaque unguessable ids returned only to the storing user; frame/duration/char/seed/batch bounds all enforced; per-hop revalidation present; fal polls execute inside the coordinator's 300 s zone timeout.

- **Pollen→dollars wording sweep (76b060efa, 92cdfd07f, 9b62de052, 6f2a69193, fe0d5e97a, 59523965f)** — every server-side hunk is string/message/comment-only; API contract names (`pollenBudget`, `questPollenOnly`, `accountBalance.*`), error codes (`QUEST_POLLEN_RESTRICTED/ONLY`, `INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE`), Stripe metadata (`packKey`), x402 purchase dedup (signed transfer `from`+`nonce`, `legacyPaymentId` fallback checked), notice dedup (`low_balance_notified_at` column), and all quest definition IDs unchanged — no renamed string feeds any idempotency key, D1 key, cache key, or webhook dedup.

ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on `rewards.idempotency_key`, claiming is a transactional `UPDATE ... WHERE claimed_at IS NULL` + `changes() = 1` credit, amounts are server constants or maintainer-only labels whitelisted to `[1,3,5,10,15,25,50]`, github-contributions verifies via a GitHub App token with `databaseId` cross-checks, and post-pay label edits cannot re-pay. 24187a4ae is a single `paidOnly: true→false` flag on flux.2-flex (stated product decision, RPM-capped).
- **Games/play (7 game commits)** — ship no in-repo code: app.json entries + README rows pointing at external URLs; ingestion `normalizeUrl`/`clean()` enforce http(s) and strip control chars; screenshot URLs restricted to github user-attachments; rendering has zero XSS sinks (no dangerouslySetInnerHTML/innerHTML/srcdoc/postMessage anywhere in pollinations.ai/src); play uses the logged-in key, no URL-param navigation.
- **Community 300 s deadline (2ed51176a) — registry update for issues 4/22:** the upstream fix binds the deadline to **response headers only** (`clearTimeout` in the `finally` once `fetch` resolves — `communityEndpoint.ts:31-36`, codified by its own test "clears the deadline after headers and keeps caller cancellation"), and neither production caller threads a caller signal (`genericOpenAIClient.ts:516-521`, `responses/client.ts:232-237` pass no `signal`), so `AbortSignal.any`'s caller half is dead code in production. Body reads (`response.text()`, whole-SSE buffering, `clone().json()`) remain time-unbounded; response **size** caps also remain absent upstream. Same root cause as issues 4/22 (not re-filed); our fork fix (issue 22 / PR #26) covers both halves. Dated update appended to issue 22's report.
- **cdde268c1 exposure note (registry update for issue 20):** the inlining commit routes user-image fetches through the hardened `fetchUserImage` (policy, manual redirects, 20 MiB caps) but extends the **deadline-less** fetch (`no signal` at `imageUrlToBase64Transform.ts:37-40`) from the niche Kimi routes onto grok-4.20/4.3/4.6, mistral-large-3 and kimi-k3.1-pro chat/Responses/Messages traffic. Same root cause as issue 20 (not re-filed); the pending issue-20 fix's site.
- **ZONOS3 below-gate notes (recorded, not filed):** the byte-unit preflight estimate resolves to the price of **one byte** (`getDefinedRequestEstimate`: `priceUnits.completionAudioTokens.quantity ?? 1`, no quantity declared) — $0.000005 reserved vs ≤$0.05 actual, bounded to one request's delta per account by the settle floor blocking follow-ups; settlement reads usage headers at ~header-arrival for binary bodies, so a destroyed mid-stream response still bills the full input text (≤$0.05, user-fairness asymmetry); no output-duration bound (10k chars ≈ 10-17 min audio per slot) — capacity observation. `speech.mjs` ffmpeg argv is spawn-array form with a validated integer sample rate (no injection); `setup-vast.sh` has no secrets and pins revisions.
- **Mistral `:aws` fallback (219aa6ce0)** — explicit costs equal to base text rates, multiplier-consistent with the base route and the old `:mistral` rows; the dropped `promptCachedTokens` only matters if Bedrock's mistral reports cache reads (unverified, cents-scale; same shape as the in-flight `solution40-fallback-cost-sheet-completeness` branch from the concurrent session).
- **MCP 404/5xx passthrough (d2294d197)** — caller-scoped (the user's own gateway response, obtainable by direct REST call with the same key); non-JSON/HTML bodies keep the canned fallback; stricter than the pre-existing 400/500/default cases. **CLI catalog estimate (e4a35ebda)** — client-side, unauthenticated public catalog first, print-only. **repo-triage workflow + issue templates** — unchanged untrusted-content flow (the recorded below-gate item); few-shot strings only.

### Verified design-gap finding without a code PR (policy decision required)

**R15-D1: referral rewards are self-dealable with any second payment instrument (~2× value on own spend).** The self-referral guard correlates only Stripe card fingerprints (`referrals.ts:46-55` — `NOT EXISTS (... stripe_card_fingerprint_attempt friend JOIN referrer ...)`); a fresh referrer account has no fingerprint at all, so the anti-join is vacuously true, and a "friend" account that buys the required >$2 via a different card, PayPal or Polar credits the referrer $2 while the friend keeps the balance — ≈$4.01 of value for $2.01 spent per cycle, bounded by payment fees and OAuth account creation. The in-code comment shows the threat was considered; instrument diversity defeats it. Fix is a product decision (raise the floor above the reward, referrer-side hold/velocity caps, or device/IP correlation) — evidence complete for that discussion; reproducible in the `test/quests.test.ts` harness with a `polar_checkout_credits` fixture.

### Verified-pending leads (mechanism verified from code this round; fix needs a product decision / next session; next free index 54)

- **Sonilo SFX `reference_video` duration uncapped** — fal bills the video's own seconds at its live price (`sonilo.ts:91-92` settles fal's billable units exactly), but nothing bounds the video length and preflight is the Tinybird mean: a 1-hour video bills **$32.40** while a $0.51 balance passes the gate (10-min → $5.40). Same class as issues 32/36 on a new path with an unbounded caller-controlled scaler. Fix needs a cap decision (audio family convention is 380 s; the worker never fetches the video, so an exact issue-36-style gate would need a bounded duration probe).
- **ElevenLabs Music v2/v2.5 reference-audio ingestion capped by bytes (20 MB) not seconds** — 20 MB @ 32 kbps ≈ 5,242 s of billable ingestion ($0.0025/s) ≈ **$13.86/req** on a mean preflight; `composition_plan` (`z.unknown()`, unvalidated) bypasses the 3-300 s output-duration validation; v2.5 inherits v2's 24,000 B/s mp3-seconds constant unverified.
- **Per-megapixel image models preflight on a one-unit estimate** — `getDefinedRequestEstimate` prices 1 MP while Azure FLUX.2 edit paths forward un-resized references (up to 8 × ~64 MP): $0.03 estimate vs ~$15.5 settlement (settle side is provider-grounded; the gap is preflight + no input resize on the FLUX.2 routes, unlike the GPT-image path).
- **Stable Audio 3 Large poll-abandonment unbilled** — submit 202 (Stability has billed the flat 26-credit generation) then 180 s poll timeout → unbilled 504; the `billedUsage` mechanism exists and is used by the fal fallback (issue-25 fix pattern).
- **GET /audio/:text bypasses the documented 10,000-char cap** on every per-character/per-byte TTS model (param validators have no max; POST-only cap) — bounded only by the ~16 KB request line; Fish Audio bills UTF-8 bytes (astral chars ×4).
- **OpenRouter Gemini TTS 502 after paid generation** when the `/generation?id=` record lags > 60 s (usage side-channel dependency; audio already delivered).

### Verified finding without a code branch (product decision required — the R3-D1 convention)

**Issue 63** — a cache-write failure after a fully-billed generation settles the charge, then fails the request with a 503 and no refund (`execute-generation.ts:148-158` awaits the settlement *before* rethrowing; the alarm catch converts it to `finish(unavailable)`; the caller paid and receives an error). Deterministic repro pinned (mock `MEDIA.put` rejection through the inline-coordinator harness); two mutually-exclusive fix directions documented (post-hoc refund/reconcile vs best-effort cache + delivery-architecture change). Filed as `issues/issue63-cachewrite-failure-bills-then-503.md`.

### Overcharge direction audited clean (subagent A) — the systematic defenses

Settlement is provider-receipt-based wherever receipts exist (fal billable-units, OpenRouter `usage.cost`, InferencePort credits, Vercel gateway receipts, minimax `providerBilling`); usage extraction carves provider detail subsets disjointly before billing (inclusive vs additive conventions detected); multipliers apply exactly once (BYOP +25% once, no double application); no minimum-charge floors, no `Math.ceil` on tokens. All five constructed margin candidates were documented intent (grok-4.7 long-context 2× tier "preserve the existing customer tier", zonos3 31.25× GPU economics, FLUX.2 whole-megapixel provider convention pinned by its own test fixtures, grok-4.20 no-Azure-cache-meter mirror, MAI streaming vs batch meters). Undercharge-direction residuals recorded below-gate for the next rate sweep: gemini-3.7/3.8-flash Vertex cache **storage** $0.5/M-token-hour vs APIDOCS' $1 (or a stale doc), gpt-image-1.5/2 cached at 25% vs the family's 10%, gemma-4-26b missing `promptCachedTokens` (issue-52 class), gemini-2.5-flash-lite audio $0.10/M on Vercel vs $0.30/M on its own OpenRouter fallback.

### Below-gate records (with evidence, not filed)

- **`hasMergedPr` id verification gap** (`enter/src/services/quests/groups/github-contributions.ts:638-648`): the one-time merged-PR reward ($5 quest pollen) is granted on a GitHub **username** search with no `databaseId` check while every sibling path verifies the immutable id — cross-account attribution + mint after a username rename/release (the round-3 rejected precondition class). Repro mirror recorded (`quests.test.ts` merged-PR test harness); fix = request `author.databaseId` in `FIRST_MERGED_PR_QUERY` and match `user.githubId`.
- **Joiner refusal inheritance** (companion to issue 1's class): sync joiners share the first submitter's persisted auth snapshot, so the owner's mid-flight 402/403 (budget depleted between submit and DO execution) is replayed to joiners whose own preflight passed — wrongful denial, not money; repro recipe recorded against `test/generation-deduplication.test.ts:147-248`.
- **`content_filter` completions cache residual** (companion to issue 40): a 200 `finish_reason:"content_filter"` body without usage is cached as an immutable free HIT — neither issue 40's filed fix nor its tests cover `content_filter`; one-line extension recipe recorded against `test/text-cache.test.ts`.

### Audited clean this round (no gate-passing finding, with the traced code)

- **Enter promise-fulfillment arithmetic (subagent B):** claim path double-claim/idempotency (D1 `claimed_at IS NULL` + `changes()=1` + UNIQUE idempotencyKey), reward-amount bounds at record+claim, repeatable-reward per-instance keys, retroactive replay skips, referral loop economics (> $2 purchase per side), x402 purchase/payment idempotency + key derivation from secret not receipt, pack pricing server-side 1:1, ledger precision (`ROUND(...,8)`, `-0` collapse), quest frontend server-fed values only — clean (the `hasMergedPr` gap above is the one exception, capped at $5).
- **Settlement state machine (subagent C):** double-settle impossible on every traced path (`detachedExecutionTracked` suppression + executor single-settle + realtime retry guard; `handleBalanceDeduction` throws rather than charging an unreserved key — failure mode is unbilled, never double-billed); cache-HIT release is a guarded no-op; fallback attempts bill exactly once at the requested listing's price; questPollenOnly × paidOnly ladder unreachable; coordinator nonce handoff non-replayable; cache-key composition complete (all output-changing params keyed).
- **Provider clients (subagent D):** SSE framing/`[DONE]` synthesis split-chunk-safe; usage-in-final-chunk disconnect billing settles full value (tee design); `messages/translate.ts` id/role-faithful; no header/URL injection from user input (Bedrock SigV4 over fixed URLs, community bearers only); systemOne rejects usage-less answers pre-billing; realtime disconnect bills exactly completed responses (one transient-D1-failure skip noted — same tradeoff as chat).

The concurrent PR #68 is a **superset** (it also covers the 3-consecutive-poll-errors 502 path) with the same receipt and rationale — cross-validated line-by-line against this session's version before discarding the local branch (never pushed). No companion filed: zero residual.
- **ElevenLabs Music ingestion (round-16 lead) — companion filed as issue 64 / PR #76.** The concurrent PR #71 caps references at 300 s **post-upload** (413 + `billedUsage` for the ingestion seconds) and validates plan totals; the provider is already paid when its cap fires, the `billedUsage` charge lands on a mean-admitted wallet (settle floor still open, issue 31), within-cap requests (300 s + 300 s = $1.50) stay ungated, and non-object/chunkless plans pass its validator silently. This session's `solution64-music-ingestion-funds-gate` (13 tests, 9 red on unpatched main — the byte-capped ~646 s reference dispatched on main and 402s with zero ElevenLabs calls post-fix; the 3,600 s plan forwarded on main and 400s post-fix; issue-36-style exact-funds gate + key-budget top-up + registry-driven strict plan validation via `durationError`) composes with #71: gate at dispatch, cap inside `generateMusic`.
- **Sonilo reference_video (round-16 lead) — retired below gate, no companion.** Vendor evidence found this round: Sonilo documents three-minute video inputs per generation on fal (1.0 announcement; the v1.1 schema page states `$0.009/s` with no explicit limit), bounding the exposure at ~180 × $0.009 = $1.62 — below the standalone gate. PR #70's settlement-side 380 s bill-and-refuse adds the product decision. The issue-36-style exact gate would need a video-duration probe the worker cannot do without fetching the whole video (the sonilo path never downloads it); recorded as the design note for any future preflight-side fix.
- **Per-megapixel multi-reference preflight (round-16 lead) — still a lead, arithmetic re-verified.** `flux.2-max`/`flux.2-pro`/`flux.2-flex`: `$0.03`/`$0.015`/`$0.05` per MP on both legs, `maxReferenceImages` 8/8/10, `getDefinedRequestEstimate` prices exactly one MP (`REQUEST_PRICE_FIELDS` first match × `quantity ?? 1`), and the download path caps bytes (20 MB) but not dimensions — PNG dimensions decouple from bytes, so the input-MP settlement is caller-controlled and unbounded pre-download. Settlement arithmetic is provider-grounded (`imageInputMegapixels ?? summed readImageDimensions`), so the user is billed what the provider bills; the platform's loss channel is the wallet overdraft on the mean admission. Not filed: provider acceptance of very-high-MP inputs is unverified (a live probe costs $0.50-8 of real provider credits — disproportionate), and the fix needs a policy decision (input-MP cap value) plus ref-count-aware estimate plumbing. Fix shape for the graduating round: cap input image megapixels at download/prepare time on the per-MP routes + thread `(reference count, requested output MP)` into the estimate. Cross-ref: issue 35's residual-gaps note deliberately left megapixel pricing unit-shaped; issue 29 is the fallback-cost split, not this.
- **OpenRouter Gemini TTS 502-after-paid-generation** — still below gate (usage side-channel dependency; audio already delivered on the paid path).

- **gpt-image-1.5/2 "cached at 25% vs family's 10%"** — reframed and killed: OpenAI's model page publishes **dual cached rates** (text $1.25 = 25% of $5; image $2.00 = 25% of $8 — the "family's 10%" comparison was a mini-only red herring), and the images API's `input_tokens_details` contains only `text_tokens`/`image_tokens` with **no `cached_tokens` field** (API reference), so the single registry cached line is inert on this path: the platform bills full rates on cached prefixes (caller-overcharge margin the API cannot observe), no APIDOCS promise covers image-model caching, and the platform never loses money. - **gemini-2.5-flash-lite audio $0.10/M (Vercel route) vs $0.30/M (its own OpenRouter fallback)** — provider side confirmed ($0.30/M via OpenRouter's public per-modality pricing; the repo's own `:openrouter:ai-studio` fallback and `:search` sheets both use $0.30), but **Vercel's own published audio rate is not observable** (marketplace page shows only the $0.10 text headline; no per-modality breakout, no public pricing API) — the understatement is only real if the Vercel gateway passes through Google's audio rate. Below gate pending Vercel-side evidence; recorded as the verification path for a future round.

### Method notes and dedup discipline

- The three fixes are companions to unmerged branches and compose with them by design: issue 71's guard is a separate function at issue 40's call site; issue 72 leaves the xAI moderation throw to issue 2's branch and the minimax/kling/grok-fal/sonilo sites to issue 25's; issue 73 adds the same optional request-bound parameter shape to `getEstimatedPrice` that solution31 adds for per-second video.
- Issue 72 deliberately does NOT settle provider-side FAILED/failed/cancelled/expired ops (a concurrent session disproved, vs fal's published docs, that fal bills FAILED jobs), pre-submission failures, or the image usage-guard family (MAI/Vercel/Vertex/OpenRouter/Alibaba "no billing metadata" throws — recorded as the named residual with the same convention issue 25 used for this sweep). `falFallbackMediaModel` non-grok download and `model3d/inferenceportClient` remain open residuals from issue 25's list.
- Issue 73's input leg is provider-grounded: BFL's FAQ documents the 4 MP per-image ceiling (inputs over 4 MP resized; generation caps at 4 MP) and Replicate's flux-2 logs show the same resize — this vendor evidence is what graduated the round-17 lead past the "policy decision" blocker. The fal-tier models (flux-3-klein, qwen-image-2.1) are excluded (quantity-scaled units, provider-reported, not request-computable).
- Leads investigated and retired this round with provider-published evidence (do not resubmit): gpt-image-1.5/2/2.5-flare cached input at 25% — **correct**: Azure's own pricing publishes cached text $1.25/M (25% of $5) and cached image $2/M (25% of $8); the registry mirrors the provider and the family's mini-at-10% is mini's own provider rate. The round-16 note was a false lead.
- The joiner-refusal-inheritance lead (round 16) was re-verified and stays below gate: the wrongful 402/403 replay to sync joiners is transient (a retry re-submits and the joiner becomes the owner), and the fix would change the coordinator's no-retry semantics.
- One parallel subagent APIDOCS-parity sweep failed on infrastructure limits twice (context deadline); the settlement audit succeeded and its 9 candidate classes were individually verified — 3 accepted (as parts of issues 72/73 and residuals), the rest deduped to the registry or retired on provider evidence.
- Upstream PR creation re-verified this round: `403 Resource not accessible by personal access token` — fork PRs #83–#85 are the live review surface.

### Rate-sweep dispositions (subagent A — six below-gate leads, all killed on provider-published evidence)

- **liquid/d1.2 missing `promptCachedTokens`** — killed: the Vercel gateway's own model sheet (`ai-gateway.vercel.sh/v1/models`) publishes no `input_cache_read` for `liquid/d1.2` (unlike `liquid/lfm2.5-24b-a2b`); the TypeSafe/SystemOne usage object is exactly `{input_tokens, output_tokens}` (vercel.com/docs/ai-gateway/sdks-and-apis/typesafe, docs.liquid.ai), and Liquid's d1-family cached rate equals the input rate anyway (liquid.ai/pricing) — the zero-fill carve can never trigger.
- **tencent/hy4-flash image input unbilled** — killed: both serving gateways (Vercel sheet; OpenRouter `openrouter.ai/api/v1/models`) publish flat per-token input for hy4-flash with no image price key, and OpenRouter's documented `prompt_tokens_details` has no `image_tokens` field — image tokens ride `prompt_tokens` at the priced rate.
- **Vercel gemini-2.5-flash-lite audio $0.10 vs $0.30** — killed: the Vercel sheet publishes no audio rate and audio is not in the model's input modalities there (flat $0.10/M input); $0.30/M is Google's/OpenRouter's rate, correctly applied only on the `:openrouter` fallback entries.
- **Kling hardcoded $0.14/unit** — killed (verified correct): fal's endpoint pages now publish kling-v3 rates ($0.084/$0.126/$0.154 standard, $0.112/$0.168/$0.196 pro) and every one is an exact multiple of $0.14 — the constant is fal's internal unit price and the seconds-equivalent billing reproduces the published dollar cost exactly.
- **OpenAI TTS code-points vs UTF-16/bytes** — killed: the repo already counts Unicode code points (`[...text].length`, test-pinned "Hi 🌻" → 4), matching OpenAI's OpenAPI `maxLength: 4096` (JSON-Schema maxLength = code points) and the "per 1M characters" unit; the byte-length computations belong to Fish/Zonos routes whose registries document per-byte units. Worst-case counterfactual ≈ $0.12/request.
- **AWS Bedrock Converse video limits** (fix-sizing input, not a lead): Nova 2 user guide publishes video inline 25 MB / **1 object**; images inline 25 MB / 5 objects; Nova v1 "a single video in the payload". Note recorded: the registry's `maxReferenceImages: 20` for nova-2-lite exceeds Nova 2's documented 5 inline images (Bedrock API-level 20 vs model-level 5 — a provider-400 risk, not a billing leak; left as a doc nit).

### Transform-fleet and schema-bounds dispositions (subagents B and C)

- **Transform fleet (B):** no new gate-passing finding. Three deterministic 500-class crash bugs recorded **below gate** (no amplification/isolate/billing effect): non-string `image_url.url` → TypeError in `imageUrlToBase64Transform` (also reachable via `/v1/responses`' untyped `z.array(z.unknown())` input); five malformed `video_url` shapes → TypeError in `mediaToBedrock` (fixed as part of issue 74's rewrite of the same lines); unbounded recursion (`sanitizeToolSchemas.sanitize` at ~6.6k frames, `countParts`) over deep caller JSON → RangeError 500 for any model (~170 KB body) — with the related note that `tools` is uncapped while legacy `functions` caps at 128. Recommended as a low-severity robustness batch, not bounty-grade.
- **Fallback amplifier retired by issue 74's cap:** the 4-model fallback list re-runs transforms per attempt with a fresh 20 MiB budget — with the video count capped at 1 the worst case is ≤ 4 fetches per request.
- **Schema-bounds sweep (C):** one gate-passing finding (issue 75) and one fix-coverage gap (issue 76). Verified clean with evidence: chat `messages`/content-part arrays (image inlining already capped 8/20 MiB; safety scan 25 parts/50 k chars), `tools` (local traversal only), `/v1/messages` (images replaced beyond the model's cap, never fetched), embeddings (batch 32; multimodal parts = issue 55's pending `MAX_EMBEDDING_PARTS`), official image `image` param (all 34 image-input models declare `maxReferenceImages` 1–16), `reference_images/videos/audios` (forwarded, never fetched), audio (one ElevenLabs call; single `reference_audio` URL), realtime (per-item regex validation), MCP (batch arrays rejected), jobs (no arrays), 3D `image` (validation only), community text/audio/embeddings endpoints (single upstream call, 5-min deadline, response arrays count-checked).

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 85 | Standalone MCP workers buffer unbounded JSON-RPC bodies before the handler runs: the **public umbrella** (`mcp.pollinations.ai` / `mcp.myceli.ai`) needs only a garbage bearer token to reach the read (bearer *presence* is the only gate; 200 req/10 s per IP), and machines/exa/ffmpeg/composio repeat the same read behind the gen gateway | High (availability — shared-isolate memory amplification; issues 12/65/75/84 class) | 3 new tests red on unpatched main (9 MiB body → 406 after full read vs 413 precheck; streamed body fully drained 32 MiB vs ≤12 MiB pulled) / green with the fix; suites mcp 12/12, ffmpeg 15/15, ask-jev 10/10, machines 8/8, exa 4/4, composio 8/8; gen typecheck + biome + wrangler dry-run clean | `solution86-mcp-worker-body-caps` | fork [#97](https://github.com/NamanSoni78/pollinationsai/pull/97) |

**Fix shape:** new `shared/mcp-request.ts` (`MCP_REQUEST_LIMIT_BYTES` = 8 MiB, `McpRequestTooLargeError`, `readMcpRequestJson` — clone read through the existing bounded reader with content-length precheck; 8 MiB keeps `deploySite`'s documented 5 MB payloads working); `shared/response-bytes.ts` param widened to `BoundedBodySource`; the five workers' pre-check/payload reads bounded, 413 over cap.

**Dedup/coordination with concurrent rounds:** ask-jev's public-domain exposure is issue 79 / PR #93 (per-IP limiter + 1 MiB probe cap) and the gen `/mcp/:serverId` gateway cap is `solution85-mcp-gateway-body-cap` (4 MiB; its issue report was still pending on `issues_mds` at this round's start) — this round's branch touches neither file. Review note recorded in issue 85 for solution85: a 4 MiB gateway cap will 413 the umbrella's documented 5 MB `deploySite` payloads routed through gen; this round chose 8 MiB accordingly.

### Surfaces verified clean this round (controls held — for the record)

| Surface | Evidence |
|---|---|
| Text SSE client-disconnect settlement | Architecturally decoupled: usage rides the provider's final SSE chunk on the **tracking tee branch** consumed inside `executionCtx.waitUntil` (`track.ts:330`), registered before the first client byte; the provider fetch has no AbortSignal (`genericOpenAIClient.ts:516-521`), so a client disconnect cannot cancel the provider stream and the settlement reads it to natural completion — the deliberate inverse of realtime's issue 66. Audio TTS bills from headers computed pre-stream; community text-proxy streams through the same tee. Residual (runtime, not code): Cloudflare's wall-clock cap on post-disconnect `waitUntil` work could kill settlement for very long generations. |
| billedUsage plumbing per route family | Image routes preserve client `billedUsage` (`throwImageError` rethrows/spreads it); audio routes have no lossy re-wrap between clients and track (`withModelFallback` rethrows the original error; lyria/sonilo are last-candidate or no-fallback so the intermediate-attempt drop at `track.ts:380` cannot hit them — and `solution86-fallback-paid-leg-settlement` addresses that drop generally); text routes preserve via `throwTextError` + `trackJsonErrorUsage`; jobs settle in the detached executor with its own track+onError. model3d's `throw3dError` still strips `billedUsage` from *non-HTTPException* carriers (latent-only after solution75 — no producer; recorded as a hardening nit with `azureFluxKontextModel.ts:157-165`'s field-by-field rewrap). |

| # | Finding | Severity | Verification | Fix branch | PR |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 105 | The enter worker had no request body limits outside sites.ts: the GitHub secret-scanning webhook buffers the full anonymous body (then `Buffer.from` copies it) **before** the signature check, and `parseFormOrJsonBody` parses unbounded bodies on `POST /api/device/code`, `/deny`, `/token` and the public `/api/oauth/token` — the enter-side instance of the filed body-limit family (all 13 prior issues live on gen/media/MCP/apps), killing the shared enter isolate (auth + account + OAuth + Stripe co-tenants) | High (availability) | 8 cases in `test/anonymous-body-limits.test.ts` — red on unpatched main (2 MiB webhook body → 401 after full buffering+copy; 128 KiB device/oauth bodies → 400 after full parse; no 413 path existed), green with the fix (413 before anything is read), 4 small-body controls byte-identical; related suites 66/66 (`github-secret-scanning`, `device-auth`, `oauth-code`, `oauth-provider`); tsc error count identical to unpatched main; biome clean | `solution105-enter-anonymous-body-limits` | fork [#119](https://github.com/NamanSoni78/pollinationsai/pull/119) |
| 106 | `MediaUpload.put` — the media worker's only generation-write path — buffered the entire upstream body (`arrayBuffer()`, no precheck, no running cap). The gen-side cap family bounded the gen read legs, but the cache write RPC buffers the whole clone: a community speech endpoint (publisher-chosen `audio/*` content-type, private free model) streams an endless body through `speechAudioCache` → `capture()` → `c.env.MEDIA.put`, OOMing the shared media isolate (co-tenant uploads/retrievals + gen's own cache reads) and parking oversized R2 objects | High (availability) | 3 cases in `media.pollinations.ai/test/media-put-cap.test.ts` — red on unpatched main (66 MiB stream: `put()` resolved after buffering the whole body and stored it; 1-byte body declared 65 MiB: resolved and stored), green with the cap (both reject before R2 is touched; small media stores byte-identically); full media suite 79/79; gen's `media-cache` wiring suite 12/12 (the fix initially tripped workerd's entrypoint-export validation in gen's service-binding harness — the cap constant is deliberately unexported, noted in the code); tsc error count identical to unpatched main; biome clean | `solution106-media-put-byte-cap` | fork [#120](https://github.com/NamanSoni78/pollinationsai/pull/120) |

Method: main-agent line-by-line diff-audit of the **full unaudited range `6824ff7b77..7e8c248eb` (11 commits)** — the Pollen→$ wording sweep (×8, apps/docs/UI text only), WalletAmount UI, the infra-watch R2-write rule, `media-upload.ts` byte-range serving (74b7abef2), the quest-label read refactor (d88831f3f), reasoning-effort remaps + Mistral developer-role remap, and the ZONOS voice clone (7e8c248eb) — plus **four parallel read-only subagent audits**: (A) the enter quest pipeline end-to-end including the new label-read path; (B) the media worker's range serving + every RPC consumer; (C) `packages/ui` + all frontends rendering GitHub-sourced content (XSS sweep); (D) gen under-covered corners + the pollinations.ai site.

### Verdict: zero new gate-passing findings

- **Edge-limiter candidates independently re-discovered** by audit D (`/openapi.json` + `/docs/open-api/generate-schema` per-request schema-regeneration fan-out; `POST /internal/generation/:id` unthrottled DO RPC churn) — verified as **exact duplicates of issue 68 / `solution57-edge-rate-limit-coverage` (PR #80, pending)** by reading both the issue and issue 97's dedup note ("issue 68 … covers `/v1/messages`, `/internal/generation/:id` and the two OpenAPI routes only"). The independent rediscovery is cross-validation of that finding's validity, not a new filing.
- **ZONOS voice clone + quest-label refactor audited clean by BOTH this session and concurrent session 30's addendum** — independent double-verification of the same verdict. The gen-side reference ingest reuses the hardened user-media fetch (URL validation, 30 s deadline, 20 MiB cap, audio content-type gate, upload-as-File rejected at the route); the GPU worker adds a 20 MiB multipart cap, reference→base64 with 400 mapping, and an ffmpeg wrapper restricted to `-protocol_whitelist file -format_whitelist wav,mp3,flac,ogg,aac,mov,matroska,webm`; the flat-fee no-input-surcharge billing matches the platform's own documented stable-audio convention.
- **Range parser** (`requestedByteRange`): safe-integer guarded on every branch, single-range only (multi-range ignored per RFC 9110 §14.2), suffix-0 → 416, end<start ignored, no negative/NaN path to R2; `refreshAgedMedia` rewrites under `onlyIf: { etagMatches }` (no resurrect); CSP sandbox + nosniff re-applied last on 200/206/416 alike; every `MEDIA.get` consumer verified arg-order-correct (jobs.ts is the only Range forwarder).

### Below-gate registry (recorded, not filed)

- **B1**: media 206/416 responses carry `Cache-Control: public, immutable` with **no `Vary: Range`** — not exploitable as deployed (zero Cache-API usage in either worker; the Cloudflare edge does not cache worker-returned 206s by default; browsers never substitute a stored 206 for a plain full GET). One-line defense-in-depth if a server-side cache layer ever lands.
- **B2**: `ctx.waitUntil(refreshAgedMedia(env, id))` has no rejection handler — log-noise parity gap with the full-GET path's `onError` threading; the response is already sent.
- **B3**: media CORS `exposeHeaders` omits `Content-Range`/`Accept-Ranges` (cross-origin JS cannot read them; `X-Content-Size` is exposed and carries the size) — parity gap only; `Range` itself is fetch-safelisted.
- **B4**: `quest-overview.tsx:1480-1482` extracts the leaderboard-nudge href from an untrusted quest description via raw regex (`description.match(/\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/)`), bypassing react-markdown's URL sanitizer — inert under React 19's `javascript:`-URL stripping plus the maintainer QUEST-label gate; sanitize via `defaultUrlTransform` (or reject non-http(s)) as one-line hardening.
- **B5**: no CSP on `pollinations.ai` (`public/_headers` covers HSTS/nosniff/XFO/RP only) nor on enter's Workers-assets SPA — defense-in-depth only: quest descriptions are link/image-stripped and 260-char truncated before render, app screenshots are sharp-re-encoded to `media.pollinations.ai` URLs, avatars are GitHub/Discord CDN.
- **B6**: `reportedIssueProposals` fixed-check (github-contributions.ts:608-614) lacks the bounty path's `baseRefName === "main"` filter — worst case an early $2 for an issue reporter in a maintainer-merged non-default-branch edge; far below gate.
- **B7** (still-live, first recorded by round 30): `/register` accepts `PLN_GPU_TOKEN` via `?token=` (secret-in-URL hygiene class; constant-time compare, scoped, rate-limited) — unchanged, below gate.

### Coverage map (clean verdicts, cross-validated with rounds 30–32)

- **Enter quest pipeline (A): 6/6 clean** — label-read refactor (query static/parameterized, amounts from maintainer-only `reward-N` labels whitelisted to [1,3,5,10,15,25,50]); mergedClosers cross-repo/nameWithOwner + `baseRefName==="main"` gating with three independent dedup layers (earned-ids, account filter, global idempotencyKey `onConflictDoNothing`); catalog anonymous-by-design returning 260-char compacted descriptions with `winnerGithubIds` stripped; frontend all-React-context (zero `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` in the frontend); GitHub App installation-token auth (no user-controllable token); `commitAuthorsQuery` numeric-only interpolation.
- **Media worker + RPC (B): 6/6 clean** — parser/headers/cache/concurrency/upload/arg-order as above; `uploadUnlistedMedia` caps enforced via `FixedLengthStream`; filename `Content-Disposition` always `encodeURIComponent`'d (no header injection); `opensInline` gate blocks html/svg-xml; HTTP upload route key-gated.
- **UI XSS (C): clean** — Markdown has no `rehype-raw`/`urlTransform` override anywhere (default sanitizer active, `javascript:` stripped, tested); `InlineLink` pairs `target=_blank` with `noopener noreferrer`; CodeBlock renders language/code as React text; `Date.parse` never throws; `authorize`/`top-up` redirect params allowlist-validated.
- **Gen corners + site (D): clean except the two issue-68 duplicates** — docs/well-known are static `?raw` imports with lookup-table 404s (no traversal); llmsTxt origin from `getPublicOrigin` (client headers ignored, per #17462); e2b ownership + capacity + funds-gating re-verified; models per-user filtering happens at response time (private entries never in shared caches); sites dispatch by exact label regex with reserved names and 5-site atomic claim; async jobs owner-checked (`status(userId)`, 64-hex unguessable ids, `Cache-Control: private, no-store` + `Vary: Authorization`); pollinations.ai worker redirects fixed-origin with URL-encoded reflection.
